# Cat Cafe test plan (vertical slice)

Owner: qa-playtester. Draft v0.4, 2026-10-26 (v0.1 to v0.3.1 the same day; v0.4 follows design v0.4, docs PR #62,
with the reviewer's N45, N46, N47 and S36 folded in). Sources:
[gdd.md](../design/cat-cafe/gdd.md) (core loop), [features.md](../design/cat-cafe/features.md) (done-when per
feature, loop script in section 8), [world-and-feel.md](../design/cat-cafe/world-and-feel.md) (v0.4 world, cafes and
UI, QA acceptance checks in section 8),
[data-model.md](../design/cat-cafe/data-model.md) (every number), the `playtest-report` skill (report and ticket
format, severity scale). QA tests, it does not fix: every failure becomes a ticket for its owner.

**Which version applies.** Design v0.3 (Gavin's answers) replaces the hold-and-release net with closing circles,
lure spots with the bait bowl, cafe level with tiers, spots with roles and the rescue hold with minigames, and adds
carry bumps, shinies, the night dealer, night income, rich customers and cat names. The engineer builds it in the
order of features.md section 1 (F22 first). Every row below names its feature; a row whose feature has not landed
yet is skipped with the reason "not built", not failed.

**Design v0.4** (Derek's rough draft playtest, 2026-10-26) jumps the queue: F34 catching stakes (the first Miss
ends the catch with no roll, any mix of Good and Perfect always catches, paws per player per stray, a Perfect charm
bonus), F35 the Alleyway behind the cafes with walk speed 20, F19 fast travel at P0, then F36 to F39 (cafe shell,
cafe life, For Rent plots, UI patterns). Rows that change say "(v0.4)" and keep the v0.3 behaviour in a "before
F34" or "before F35" clause, so the same table works on either build.

**Design v0.5** (Gavin's round 2, 2026-10-27, features.md section 2c, docs PR #122; v0.5.1 adds the reviewer's S44, S45 and N84 to N86, docs PR #128) changes rows that are built or
in progress: F40 a missed cat sprints `fleeStuds` (12 for a Common up to 32 for a Mythical) away and anyone can try
again with no limit, replacing F34's paws; F41 Shove inside catching areas replaces F29's Bump prompt; F42 gold
coats and a 5% gold chance (called shiny until Derek's 2026-10-27 rename); F43 one dealer stock for every player and server in a window; F44 the cafe closes at
night and the bait bowl moves to the back step. Rows that change say "(v0.5, F4x)" and keep what `main` does now
in a "before F4x" clause. F40 (engineer PR #130), F41 (PR #139), F42 (PR #138), F43 (PR #141) and F44
(PR #144) are on `main`, so their rows state the build; the v0.4 paws, the F29 bump numbers, the per-player dealer
stock, the night owls and the front garden bowl are retired.

**Town v3.1 and the v0.12 and v0.13 builds** (2026-10-28 00:00 to 02:30, QA PRs #431 and #449 and the 02:30
catch-up). The town is the compass (F76 and F77, [town-layout-v3.md](../design/cat-cafe/town-layout-v3.md)): the
Alley Square, the street arch and the manhole are gone, so rows that named them use the Town Square's side arches,
the `nook` points at the alley ends and the gates off the back alleys (S44). On `main`: F55 getaway; F75 cat levels
with the hover card; F72's intro (nine shots in about 24 s; since #448 and #453 Skip shows at once and its bar fills
over 5 s); the v0.13.1 bait prices (#415); waves of 6, 5, 5, 4 and 4 strays (#426, #428); rich tips paid and
popping "+$X" only when claimed (#428, #439); food into the bowl from anywhere (#428, #420); only Mythical strays
making a sound (#430); only My Cafe keeping a marker (#432); strays stopping at any visible model and the cafe
tables in two columns (#433); the green flash on placed decor (#434); the v0.13.6 prices (#437, #441: Tiers 2 to 5
cost 8,000, 80,000, 700,000 and 5,000,000, Tables from `$150`, the Bait bowl `$600`, income unchanged); a carrier's
death dropping the cat (v0.13.3, #438); every area rolling the Alleyway's odds with farther areas' cats earning more
(v0.13.7, #446: x1, 1.5, 2.25, 3.5 and 5); the stray rarity gem (#447); the Town Square set (#444); solid area sets
with climbable ladders (#452); water splashes with a soft step for players (#455, #456); F64 potions and the Mystery Visitor on both sides
(engineer PR #459 with the UI's #442 and #451; the v0.13.8 rule refusing a drink that would pass 60 minutes is in, and since #464 v0.13.9's 10 golden fish for a
gift spin opened with the spin bank full); F80 door sets and cat flaps on both sides (engineer PR #462 with the UI's
#454 and the flash, #457: twelve pieces a crate, placeholder leaves and flaps until the models land); the Park's
invisible walls on its hedge rows and outer walls, with hedges 8 to 8.8 high (#466); F60 night at full pay with
food set at night waiting half (#482, #484, #485, #487); F61 tip piles on the tables and rich visitors worth 120 s
at 10% a minute (#486, the till's Collect #488). With F60 and F61 in, `Pace.spec` re-times Tier 2 at about 15
minutes (section 6). Since then: intro v2 (#471, the puff #473), ten shots in 25.5 s where your own avatar catches
the Tuxedo, carries it home and gives it the waiter job; F57 the longer chase, the misser's 3, 6 and 10 s waits and
the Startled slow (#479, the card's countdown #481 and #483); F58 a wave's Mythical told to the whole server (#480);
F59 the dealer's 0 to 4 of each food every 5 minutes (#489, the screen #492); F62 the cat lock's server half (#493);
F81 for the Park, 160 x 120 (#467), and the Woods, 240 x 240 (#474), with near and deep cat spots, the Great Oak's
top as a rare nook, the tree house branch trap and perched strays that stay put after a miss (#475, #478); and
invisible caps on every area wall up to 30 studs (#469); F63 the Cat Collector on both sides (engineer PR #499, UI
PR #501; a placeholder `CatCafe_NPC_Collector` until the model lands); F67 a dropped cat walking back to where it
was caught (engineer PR #502, perches review S88); the opened crate on the Market counter with its lid (VFX PR #525,
models #524); a grey gem over Common strays too, and the stuck mark only in view with the cat's gem over it (VFX PR
#543, Derek 12:13 to 12:15); F83 harder catching and rescues (engineer PR #550, data-model v0.13.15: the net's
windows times the stray's `catchWindow` 0.9 to 0.5, the tree's falling cat too, and faster bars with narrower zones).
F81 for the Sewer, 180 x 180 (engineer PR #497, set #491), and the Neighbourhood, 280 x 280 (engineer PR #508,
set #505), so every side area is at its v3.2 size.

**Since then** (2026-10-28 to 2026-11-02; QA's catch-up on 2026-11-02) these are on `main` too, so their rows state
the build: F79 bedtime on both sides (client #429, server #517); the F78 catalog switch (engineer PR #477: `calico`,
`sphynx` and `midnight` left the Alleyway and a spare cat never spawns by itself, so rows that spawn one with
`Debug.spawnStray` use `binbag`, `taxi` and `neon`, the cats in their slots); F84 the hometown and the Cat Cannon
(engineer PR #643, models #584, UI #575; S58); F85 the long Park, Neighbourhood, Woods and Sewer (engineer PRs #816,
#817, #858 and #865; S59) and the Alleyway as one lane with four low blocker rows (engineer PRs #867 and #877 on
Model-Agent's #864 and #875); and F86 keep up (engineer PR #578), which F95 turned off for Legendaries and Mythicals
(engineer PR #656, economy-ladder.md section 2), so only the shop-only cats use it (S60). With F84 in, the town has
no gates and no catching area: every area is reached only by the Cat Cannon and walked from its camp, and `Travel`
takes only `"home"` and `"cannon"` (an area id or `"alley"` answers "Hop in the Cat Cannon to fly there"). So S2's
gates, S44's walks to the gates and its Alley press, S55's starts at the gates and F19's area ids read through S58:
start at the area's camp, and a locked area is a card missing from the cannon picker.

## 1. What runs where

Code and these docs live in the GitHub repo (`derekburnz/Cat-Cafe`, renamed from `purr-pot`), the only copy.
QA branches from `main`, keeps to `games/cat-cafe/tests/`, `games/cat-cafe/tools/cloudcheck/` and
`docs/playtests/`, and opens a pull request.

| Layer | Where | When | How |
|---|---|---|---|
| Unit tests of pure modules (`tests/specs/*.spec.luau`) | Cloud threads, or any PC with Node 20 | Every change under `src/shared`, `src/server/World/Layout.luau` or the pure client `Audio` and `Effects` logic, before every review | `cd games/cat-cafe/tools/cloudcheck && npm test` |
| Strict type check and lint, cloud stand-in | Same | Same | `npm run analyze` (game), `npm run analyze -- --tests` (specs too) |
| The real done gate | Derek's PC, Rokit toolchain, and CI | Before any task is called done | `cd games/cat-cafe && ./check.ps1` (Windows) or `./check.sh` |
| Unit tests inside Studio (real Roblox globals) | Derek's PC | Before every playtest build | Section 3.2 |
| Automated Studio playtest over Studio MCP | Derek's PC, driven by Claude Code | After every feature lands, before every friend playtest | Section 4 |
| Manual checklist, phone and PC | Derek, then friends | Every friend playtest round | Section 5 |
| Ten minute loop script (Phase 2 exit) | Derek first, then two friends | End of Phase 2 | Section 6 |
| Friend feedback | Google Form or Discord | Every round | [feedback-form.md](feedback-form.md) |

Results of every run go to `docs/playtests/YYYY-MM-DD.md` in the `playtest-report` format, evidence to
`docs/playtests/evidence/YYYY-MM-DD/`.

**Live version.** Cat Cafe is published at roblox.com/games/119569792798199 (version 24 at 2026-10-28 01:17 UTC).
Since 2026-10-28 the `publish` workflow runs after every merge to `main` and moves running servers onto the new
version (PR #395), so the live game follows `main` within minutes. Any thread republishes with Actions > publish >
Run workflow. Every report names the commit its Studio build came from (`git log -1 --format=%h`) and, for a run on
the live game, the published version (Creator Hub, the place's version history), so a ticket can be matched to a
build. The published place never holds `ReplicatedStorage.Tests` or `StarterPlayerScripts.TestsAbuse`: test runs
use a dev copy built from `tests.project.json`.

## 2. Preconditions

1. Commit first. Open the **dev copy** of the place, never the published one (studio rule 1).
2. Studio MCP on: Assistant > ... > Manage MCP Servers > Enable Studio as MCP server. In Claude Code run
   `list_roblox_studios` and keep the `studio_id`; every Studio tool call takes it.
3. Rojo: `rojo serve default.project.json` for playtests, `rojo serve tests.project.json` for unit tests in
   Studio and the abuse pass (same tree plus `ReplicatedStorage.Tests` and `StarterPlayerScripts.TestsAbuse`).
   Never publish a place that contains either.
4. Data: with **Enable Studio Access to API Services** off, ProfileStore runs in mock mode, so every play session
   is a fresh account (good for onboarding tests) and nothing persists (so skip persistence steps and expect
   `dataSaves = false` in the state). Turn it on only for the persistence, offline, bait and dealer rejoin steps,
   using a dev-only DataStore name.
5. Phone checks use Studio's Device emulator (Test tab) at a 390 x 844 phone preset; multi-player checks use
   Test > Clients and Servers with 2 players. Latency checks use File > Studio Settings > Network > Incoming
   Replication Lag (set it back to 0 afterwards).
6. Debug hooks (Studio-only, `src/server/Services/Debug.luau`, every call errors outside Studio). Reach them
   through the `CatCafeDebug` bridge that `Main.server.luau` puts in ServerStorage (PR #50), from the command bar
   in Server view or from `execute_luau` on the server while playing:
   `game.ServerStorage.CatCafeDebug:Invoke("setPhase", "night")`, `:Invoke("setCoins", nil, 5000)`,
   `:Invoke("snapshot")`. Never `require` the Debug module there: those contexts get a fresh copy of every module
   whose services never started (`profile not loaded`, a clock nobody runs). Steps below write
   `Debug.name(args)` as shorthand for `CatCafeDebug:Invoke("name", args)`. `player` may be a Player, a UserId, a
   name, or nil for the first player.
   - On `main` now: `snapshot(player?)` (authoritative `PlayerState`, settled first), `setCoins(player?, n)`,
     `setLastTick(player?, secondsAgo)`, `resetProfile(player?)`, `setTier(player?, tier)`,
     `giveLure(player?, lureId, count?)`, `setLastSeen(player?, secondsAgo)` (returns the offline amount and shows
     the popup), `setSaveVersion(player?, version)`, `giveCat(player?, catId, rescued?)`,
     `setUpgrade(player?, id, level)`, `spawnStray(catId, areaId?, gold?)` (returns the StrayId; the forced roll since PR #119, named gold in PR #181), `setPhase("day"
     or "night")`, `clock()`, `spawnStuckCat(catId, rescueSpotId?, gold?)` (`rescue_alley_1` by default, the Park
     trees are `rescue_park_1` and `rescue_park_2`), `richTip(player?, staySeconds?)` (a rich tip now, returns the
     amount, PR #123), `counts(areaId?)`, `dataSaves()`,
     `baitReady(player?)` (brings the bowl's cat now and returns its catId, PR #108), `teleport(player?, areaId)`
     (onto an area's first spawn point, past its gate), `tackle(player?, "loose" or "held" or nil)` (a real
     Tackle press through every check, the roll forced when named; F74 replaced `shove`, engineer PR #363: face
     the target within the 12 stud lunge first, and the lunge itself only plays from the real button) and `knockLoose(player?)` (drops the carried cat with
     nobody barred; both PR #139) and `renameCat(player?, name, uid?)` (RenameCat with every check and the real
     filter, a working cat by default, PR #146), `telemetry(count?)` (the last analytics events, one line each:
     user, kind, name, value, fields) and `onboarding(player?)` (the funnel step and held steps; both PR #155), `tipJar(player?, minutes)` (the Tip
     Jar's session starts `minutes` earlier, so its steps unlock now; returns the session minutes, PR #206),
     `fillIndex(player?, areaId, count?, gold?)` (the area's first `count` cats, all of them when nil, into the
     Index, and into the gold Index too with `gold`, without giving any cats; returns the page's count, PR #211),
     `spins(player?, banked, freeDue?)` (sets the banked spins, with `freeDue` makes today's free spin due again and
     clears the spin cooldown; returns the spins available, PR #223), `bugReports(count?, day?, studio?)` (the
     newest `count`, default 20, bug reports of a UTC day `"YYYYMMDD"`, oldest first, one line each; with `studio`
     the Studio playtests' `s_` reports; needs API access on, PR #265), `givePotion(player?, potionId?, count?)`
     (into the bag, every potion when the id is nil), `potionLeft(player?, potionId, seconds)` (a potion's time
     left, 0 ends it within a second; returns the time before) and `mystery(player?, kind?)` (a Mystery Visitor sits
     down now with a gift of that kind, `"potion"`, `"lure"`, `"fish"` or `"spin"`; returns `"kind id xN"`; all
     three engineer PR #459). With F94 part 1 (engineer PR #672, merged 2026-11-02): `spinSlice(player?, slice)` (the next spin
     lands on that slice; `nil` goes back to random). `setFish(player?, n)` sets the golden fish in the wallet.
     With F94 part 2 (engineer PR #679): `freshCatch(player?)` (forgets the player's Fresh Catch buys and alert for
     this window; returns `"<window>: itemId=amount ..."`). In Studio the server prints a `[Catch]`
     line per graded circle, a `[Rescue]` line per judged rescue press and a `[BugReport] <json>` line per
     accepted bug report.
   - Still asked for (section 8): gold on `giveCat` and a dealer window offset. Steps marked (hook) use these;
     without the hook the step is skipped, not failed.

## 3. Unit tests

### 3.1 What is covered

Every spec in `games/cat-cafe/tests/specs` has a row below or is named in a feature row further down;
`npm run plancoverage` in tools/cloudcheck lists any that are not (rows added 2026-11-02 for 45 specs come from each
spec's header comment).

| Spec | Checks | Source |
|---|---|---|
| `TestKit.spec` | The harness itself. If this fails, trust nothing else in the run. | |
| `Catalog.spec` | Cats, rarities, spots, upgrades, decor, lures, areas: ids snake_case and unique, odds sum to 100, model names from ids; since v0.13.7 (engineer PR #446) one set of odds in every area and each cat's income its rarity's times its area's multiplier (QA edges: every area farther out pays more, a farther cat never earns less than the same rarity nearer, a spare cat pays by its `incomeArea` once F78 lands) | data-model 1 to 9 |
| `Catching.spec` | Odds tables mapped onto rolls and swept 20,000 times, area filtering, uniform pick, rarity ladder fallback, rescue odds, percent text; `spreadPick` (engineer PR #368, Derek 22:38): a free spawn point at least StraySpawnSpacingStuds 12 flat from every stray in the area, likelier the bigger the gap, else the farthest, evenly in an empty area, nil with no points, a ten-cat Park wave never under 12 studs while a far point is free; QA: 500 seeded areas at four rolls each always give a far enough point when one is free and else the farthest, and a sweep of 12,000 rolls lands on each point in proportion to its gap capped at 60 (exactly 12 counts, 11.99 does not); F57 (engineer PR #479): sprints of 24, 32, 40, 52 and 64 studs, the misser's 3, 6 then 10 s by misses in a row, the streak starting again 60 s after the last miss; `spawnPool` (engineer PRs #467 and #475): a Rare or rarer stray takes a free `rare` point first, never a Common or a carried cat sent home, Epic and rarer only `nook` or `deep` points where an area has them (none free: none) and any free point where it has no zones; `waveShoutOut` (F58, engineer PR #480) names the area and not the cat, says gold, and is nil for anything but a Mythical. QA (F57, F58): the UI's StraySelfRetrySeconds is the first step and the steps rise; the longest wait for any rarity (64 studs at 26, the 1.5 s rest and 10 s) is well under the 60 s reset, so a player who retries the moment they may keeps the streak; the misser always waits longer than everyone else; Startled 12 under carry 16 under walk 20; every area's Mythical line names its area and no Catalog cat; F67 (engineer PR #502): a cat walking home (8) is slower than even a Startled player, home is well inside grab reach and the 60 s fallback outlasts a walk down a whole back alley | data-model 8 to 10c |
| `CatchFeel.spec` | F83's feel in numbers (QA, data-model 10 and 10b v0.13.15): from the build's own `Circles.windowsFor`, each rarity's circles, the real bar (`RescueMarker`, speed, zone width and tolerance), `Rescue.hitsNeeded` and RescuePatience, the chance a try nets a stray matches the design's table within a point (Basic Net 95 / 85 / 67 / 44 / 21% and Master Net 100 / 99 / 97 / 88 / 69% at 50 ms presses; 84 / 63 / 39 / 18 / 6% and 98 / 92 / 80 / 59 / 33% at 70 ms), and so does a bar rescue (100 / 100 / 97 / 73 / 37% at 50 ms, 100 / 99 / 77 / 37 / 11% at 70 ms); a Basic Net Common stays at 94% or better (80% at 70 ms); every better net helps at every rarity and every rarer cat is harder; every hit and spare paw fits inside the 15 s deadline; the tree's falling cat, one circle on the Pro Net times its own `catchWindow`, stays over 85% and never harder than the same cat's net catch | data-model 10 and 10b, F83 |
| `Waves.spec` | Whole sunrise waves on the real layout (QA; F35, v3.1 and F81), placed as `CatService.spawnWave` places them: the wave rolled, the rarest first, each stray through `spawnPool` and `spreadPick` with the strays already standing. 160 fresh waves per area, half at the real odds and half at even odds so Epic and rarer come up, place every stray, Epic and rarer only on `nook`, `deep` or `rare` points; in 300 Woods waves the Great Oak's top holds the wave's rarest stray whenever it is Rare or better and stays empty otherwise, about 73% of waves at the real odds (town layout 8d); with up to every point held from the night, a stray waits only when no point its rarity may take is free. Hand mutants (the Epic rule off, the rare zone ignored) fail it | data-model 9, town-layout-v3 7e and 8d |
| `Economy.spec` | Cafe multiplier, cat rate with favorite and variant, income sum, lazy settle, offline gain and cap, affordability, blockers in gate-before-coins order, seats, counts, the loop script in numbers (QA: its balance guards read the Catalog's prices since v0.13.6; Tier 2 takes more than 10 minutes on cafe income alone and every rank up costs at least five times the one before) | data-model 5, 6, 11, 14; features 8 |
| `Pace.spec` | The loop script's pace in numbers (QA, F60 and F61's "QA re-times Tier 2", F95's checks 3 and 7): a second by second run of a new player on the real shared code (Economy's income, settle, XP and blockers, the Clock, the Catalog's prices, odds, first catch rewards, Tip Jar and Index prizes, EquipBest, Catching and CatStats), joining at a random point of a running server's day: the tutorial Common at 75 s, 4 or 5 Alleyway trips a day while the sunrise wave lasts (a stray picked at random from the wave, which holds an Epic or rarer since F97, `Catching.waveRare`), each stray netted with presses spread about 50 ms either side of the target (graded by `Circles` on the net level's windows times the stray's `catchWindow`, F83), a miss costing the real chase (`Catching.fleeUntil`, the misser's 3, 6 and 10 s steps, the walk over at the Startled speed first), a late trip delaying the next and a stray still loose at the dusk banner left behind, home between trips and all night, every tip pile paid the moment it appears (F97) and every rich visitor collected at home, bait kept going, the rank up the moment it is allowed and otherwise the cheapest open shop item, or only the tables and the bait bowl. The median of 40 seeded runs reaches Tier 2 in 10 to 20 minutes, and the cats are ready before the coins; a shakier player (presses within about 70 ms) too. F95 (2026-11-02): the same player runs on to Tier 5, its trips going to the newest area, Equip best after each new cat and rank up, every Index milestone claimed, the bowl fed the best shop food whose price at its tier (`Economy.foodPrice`, F97) is at most a minute of income and the coin decor shop in the shop until F97 moved it into the Classic Crate; in one go, or an hour a day with `Economy.offlineGain` paid at each join, or with a Money pass (`Economy.setPasses`); Luck from the Lucky Cat and Lucky Days (F88) and each cat's coat rolled with `Catching.rollCoat`, rainbow then gold (F99). The median of 10 seeded runs reaches Tier 5 in 8 to 12 hours of play in one go and on day 4 to 6 at an hour a day, and over 2 hours with 4x Money and over an hour with every paid bonus (24x) | data-model 10, 11 and 12, features 8, F60, F61, F83, F88, F95, F97, F99 |
| `CatStats.spec` | Charm roll and stars, level and XP, rating, sort order, view, migration of v1 cats, rescued flag; F75 cat levels (engineer PR #390): XP to the next level CatXpBase 120 x n up to CatMaxLevel 50, kept in fractions so a resting cat's quarter a second adds up; each level +2% (CatLevelBonus 0.02, 1.98x at 50), so a perfect level 50 Tabby rates 2.376, a Ninja Cat 59.4 and a gold Wizard Cat 267.3; settle gives every owned cat XP, 1.5 a second in its favorite role, 1 in another and 0.25 resting, the same for every rarity and by night, an unknown spot counting as resting, and a long settle counts every level gained in `stats.catLevelUps`; `remapLevel` moves an old 10-level cat to 1 + ceil(2.5 x (L - 1)) (level 10 to 24) without lowering its rating; migrate keeps charm, remaps old levels, defaults missing fields and clamps nonsense; F62 (engineer PR #493): every gold cat and every Legendary and Mythical arrives locked, the rest and unknown ids do not, and the view sends `locked` as a boolean, false when missing | data-model 4, 4b v0.12 |
| `EquipBest.spec` | Best placement against brute force, favorite bonus, locked spots; `restAll` (Unequip All, engineer PR #175) rests every working cat, counts them, leaves income 0 and a clean slate Equip Best places optimally from | data-model 4b |
| `Clock.spec` | Phase at any time, countdown without jumps, start of each phase | data-model 9 |
| `Config.spec` | Every constant in data-model section 17 by name and value, no undocumented constant, internal ordering; F55 (engineer PR #392): the Catch Rush (CatchRushWalkSpeed 24) outruns a walking player (NormalWalkSpeed 20) and the getaway push (CatchBurstStuds 8) stays inside its own radius (CatchBurstRadiusStuds 10) | data-model 17 |
| `Guard.spec` | Engineer (PR #169): `Guard.id` passes the uid shape `CatStats.uidFromGuid` makes, the starter, catalog, spot and stray ids and an id exactly `MaxIdLength` long, and drops malformed ids. QA edges: every catalog id and rescue spot id, 500 seeded `uidFromGuid` uids (12 characters, distinct, all pass), a loaded save of 40 GUID-shaped uids made before the fix (all pass, no cat lost), and upper case, control and non-ASCII ids dropped | laptop run 2026-10-27 |
| `Signal.spec` | Connect, disconnect, once, disconnect all, one erroring handler does not stop the rest | |
| `AudioLogic.spec` | Sound rules (sound-designer, PR #64 and #69; QA edges): voice limits and the minimum gap, pitch ranges, music and ambience by phase and zone, moment lookups, the shout-out step per rarity in `Catalog.rarityOrder`, the nearest world loops within a radius (the edge counts, a max of 0 keeps none), the volume sliders' squared gain (PR #218; QA: never down as the slider rises), the hold fill speed (0.5 s plays at 0.96, clamped 0.5 to 2), the zone chimes and the stray shimmer (PR #227, cases from the sound thread; Mythical only since Sound-Agent PR #430; QA: the chime agrees with the zone names `Wayfinding` reads (the zone chip itself went in UI PR #408) on every Catalog area and the two chimes mirror each other, the hold speed never rises with a longer hold), every cue on a known channel with asset ids only, every P0 cue present; QA (PR #268): a resting cafe cat purrs on the sitting body (Pose "sit") all day and on the lying body only in a loaf, never at night, and any other pose, a number or a table counts as none; the v0.8 cues (Sound-Agent PR #303; QA edges): the dusk bell counts down to its warning before the day ends, never below 0, and is gone a second late, off day or with no end time; the Mythical sting for Mythical in any letter case (the old Secret too) and the plain shout-out for every other rarity, an unknown name or nil; a carry start counts as fresh from 2 s before now to 1 s after, never for NaN, infinities or a non-number; QA (Sound-Agent PR #370): a tackle's `TackleAt` or `TackleMissAt` only within its 0.6 s window and the carry's 2 s with no window, a clock up to 1 s ahead fresh at any window, a non-number never; a tip pile cue only when `TipPiles` falls, the Collector and Mystery Visitor cues only when `CollectorUntil` or `MysteryGiftAt` goes from 0 to above 0 (a gift clearing is silent: `giftOpen` plays with the MysteryGiftResult card), nothing for any other attribute; every cue they name is in the cue table; QA (PR #430, review N234): a Mythical stray's `rareNotice` plays at 30 studs and not at 31, stays noticed to 45 and forgets past it, plays once per approach on a walk in, part way out and back, and never for a distance of NaN or infinity; QA (F84, Sound-Agent PR #562): `inWild` is false everywhere in the hometown with its back yards and backdrop ring (400 x 400 round the centre) and true at every corner of all five areas at their F85 sizes and the end of each nearest the town, at the spec's WildAreaDistance 2000 and at the build's once Config has one; the line is half the distance from (0, 0) in every direction, 1000 itself town; no number, zero, a negative distance or NaN is never wild, nor a spot it cannot read. Hand mutants (`>=`, a third, no type check, no zero check, X only) fail it. `travelCue` (PR #574) against the rule said another way for every destination, in town or wild, hopping or not: a fresh `CloudHopAt` is always the cloud hop, the Cannon button always poofs out of a hop, any other trip from a wild area (Home, the sweep's home, an id it does not know) is the cloud hop and every trip in town poofs; both cues are in the cue table. Rescues (Sound-Agent PR #765): every rescue cue and `teamRescue` play from the Rescue sheet in its order, each of the four traps has its own hit cue and nothing else gets one, and the hit pip climbs one step a hit to every rarity's `rescueHits` in `Catalog` without passing a Mythical's 2.25 (QA edges; a Mythical at 8 hits fails them). F90 cafe orders (Sound-Agent PR #784): only the espresso machine pours, the ding steps oven 1, espresso 2, counter 3, the pour, ding, clink and bag drop play from the Cafe sheet and the sweep from `tipPile`'s file; QA edges keep all five 3D within 35 studs with a gap and at most two voices, and pick nothing for any name but a station's exact one | sound list 2 to 6 |

| `BugReport.spec` | What the server keeps of a bug report (engineer PR #265; QA edges): only the first 2,000 bytes read, so a bad byte past them is never seen; the text trimmed with its newlines, control characters out and a tab turned into a space, refused when it is not a string, invalid UTF-8 or under 3 bytes once cleaned, and cut to 500 bytes on a character boundary rather than refused; the kind `bug`, `stuck` or `idea`, anything else a bug; client error lines 1 to 3, read to 800 bytes, newlines made spaces, cut to 200, bad ones dropped, never a refusal; keys `r_<YYYYMMDD UTC>_<os.time()>_<userId>`, `s_` from Studio. QA: 2,000 seeded random reports mixing ASCII, accents, euro signs, emoji, no-break spaces, U+0085, bidi overrides, tabs, carriage returns, NUL, escape, DEL, bad bytes, overlong forms, surrogates and cut-short characters match a reference that walks code points instead of patterns, for the text and for error lines; every kept text is valid UTF-8 of 3 to 500 bytes with no control character but a newline and no space at either end, unchanged when cleaned again, and every error line has no newline at all; the end is trimmed again after the cut; a 200,000 byte flood of control characters is answered in well under a second; the toasts are the contract's words; a day's keys sort by time for any user id (a Studio test player's negative id and 2^53 - 1 included), stay inside the DataStore's 50 character limit, and Studio keys never share a prefix with live ones | bug-reports.md section 2 |
| `BugReportLogic.spec` | The Report a bug window's rules (UI PR #267; QA edges): Send off under 3 bytes once trimmed, the box and counter in bytes up to 500 (an emoji counts 4), broken UTF-8 never sent, a cut never splits a character and stops at the first broken byte, Bug, Stuck and Idea with Bug first and anything else a bug, the newest 3 client errors of 200 bytes. After UI PR #270 every rule is `shared/BugReport`'s: Send lights only when `BugReport.text` keeps the text (so "a\rb" and "a\0b" stay off) and sends what it keeps, the cut is `BugReport.cut`, the chips follow `BugReport.Kinds`, error lines are kept as `BugReport.errorLine` keeps them, and a report settles only on an exact `BugReport.Messages` toast (Thanks closes, the three refusals keep the words, any other toast is ignored). QA adds no edge: the rules are the server's own, which `BugReport.spec` checks | bug-reports.md section 1 |

Not covered by unit tests, by design: anything that needs DataStores, Players, RemoteEvents, TextService,
physics or the world. `DataService`, `BugReportService`, `CatService`, `CafeService`, `Net`, `WorldBuilder` and all of `src/client`
except the pure `Audio/Logic`, `Audio/Cues`, `Effects/Logic`, `Effects/Presets` and the `UI/Components` modules
`BugReportLogic`, `CatCardLogic`, `CatLock`, `CatRoomList`, `CatVariant`, `CodeEntry`, `CrateLogic`, `DealerRows`, `DecorPieces`, `FoodGrade`, `FoodOdds`, `IndexRewards`, `IntroLogic`, `MarketLogic`, `NightClock`, `PromptKey`, `RichTipState`, `SettingsList`, `ShortNumber`, `TackleLogic`,
`SpinLogic`, `StaffSlots`, `TipJarLogic` and `Wayfinding` are Studio-only and covered by sections 4 to 6.

### 3.2 Running them

Cloud or PC: `cd games/cat-cafe/tools/cloudcheck && npm install --no-bin-links && npm test`. Exit code 1 on any
failure; `--verbose` prints one line per test, `--filter Economy` runs one spec, `--json report.json` writes a
machine readable summary.

Studio: serve `tests.project.json`, then in the command bar or via `execute_luau`:

```lua
local run = require(game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Tests.StudioRunner)
local s = run(nil, { verbose = true })
return string.format("%d passed, %d failed, %d skipped", s.passed, s.failed, s.skipped)
```

Pass criteria: 0 failed in both places. A spec that passes in the cloud but fails in Studio (or the reverse)
is a ticket against the harness, owner QA.

## 4. Automated Studio playtest (Claude Code over Studio MCP)

Conventions: `get_console_output` after every step, any new error or warning is recorded with its full text.
`screen_capture` after every step that changes what the player sees; save to
`docs/playtests/evidence/YYYY-MM-DD/<step>.png`. Read authoritative numbers with
`game.ServerStorage.CatCafeDebug:Invoke("snapshot")` from `execute_luau` on the server and compare with the HUD. Studio's built-in `subagent` tool (playtest type) can run 4.1 end to end once
it is scripted; keep the manual tool sequence below as the reference.

### 4.2 Circle feel and latency (F26, phone emulator and PC)

The designer's feel targets (data-model section 10, v0.5): a new player with a Basic Net catches a Common in a
try or two nearly every time; a Rare or Epic feels tense but fair; a Mythical with a Basic Net is a real chase (five
quick uneven circles, and a 32 stud sprint after each miss); a Master Net makes it fair. Run each line 10 times and
record the grade of every circle (the server's `[Catch]` lines), the tries used and whether it was
caught.

| Line | Setup (hooks) | Record | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 New player, Common | Fresh profile, Basic Net, `Debug.spawnStray("ginger", "alley")`, a person who has not played it | Grades, catches out of 10, tries per catch | Caught within three tries nearly every time: 9 or more of 10 |
| C2 Mythical, Basic Net | `Debug.spawnStray("midnight", "alley")`, net 0 | Grades per circle, how uneven the five shrink times look | Perfect on all five is rare; uneven speeds are visible |
| C3 Mythical, Master Net | Same with `Debug.setUpgrade(player, "net", 3)` | Grades, final chance | Feels fair: steady speeds, most circles Good or better |
| C4 Latency | Incoming Replication Lag 0.1 s, then 0.25 s, Common, net 0; record `player:GetNetworkPing()` on the server at each setting | Perfect rate pressing on the visual target, and the ping the server saw | The server subtracts half the ping, capped at 0.15 s. If the ping reflects the simulated lag, 0.1 s should grade like the 0 s run and 0.25 s should grade late. Record, do not ticket: Studio's lag setting may not match a real phone on mobile data, so the friend round on phones is the real test |
| C5 Touch | Phone emulator, tap the action button | Circle placement (x, y spread), button size, whether the thumb hides the ring | No circle under the thumb zone; button at least 44 px |
| C7 Sure catch (v0.4) | 20 Commons and 5 Rares; press every circle inside the good window, mixing Good and Perfect (by hand, or the abuse client's on-target presses offset by up to 0.08 s) | Catches out of 25, `charmGain` per catch | 25 of 25 caught, no roll; `charmGain` is 4 per Perfect, doubled when every circle is Perfect (world-and-feel 8.2) |
| C6 Phone on mobile data | A real phone on mobile data, then on Wi-Fi, a published test place (not Studio), C1 and C2 again | Grades per circle and the ping the server saw | Phone ping wanders 30 to 50 ms against the 45 ms Basic Perfect window (data-model 10). If Good is the best a careful player gets on mobile data, file a balance note for the designer with the numbers |

If the Basic Net feels mean on touch, the designer's rule is to widen `good` before anything else; file it as a
balance note with the numbers, owner designer.

### 4.3 Two player checks (Clients and Servers, 2 players)

1. Same stray: A starts a catch, B triggers the prompt a second later. Expected: B is refused while A's schedule
   runs; only one `CatchResult` and at most one carry. (F26.)
1b. Chase after a miss (v0.5, F40; world-and-feel 8.1): A misses circle 1 of a Rare (`Debug.spawnStray("calico")`).
   Expected: the catch ends at once with no roll and the cat sprints about 16 studs away from A, faster than A
   walks, without leaving its area; while it runs and rests 1.5 s (`FleeingUntil`) both A's and B's `GrabBegin` are
   refused "It's running!"; then B can try at once and A after 1 s (S44), and A can miss and retry five times in
   a row. In a deliberate-miss loop by A, B gets the next try within 1 s of every stop. Each run ends on open
   floor a player can reach, never inside a wall strip or a prop (N84). Since engineer PR #433 a stray stops just short
   of anything a player can see, the area sets' fences, benches and stalls that do not collide included, never
   halfway into one; a stray that lands against a decoration still runs away from it on its next flee; once in each
   area set.
2. Tackle, 20 hits (F41 as v0.11 F74, engineer PR #363, UI PR #382; world-and-feel 8.3). A carries a cat in the
   Alleyway at least 3 s into the carry and outside A's safe zone; B stands 4 to 10 studs from A, faces A and
   presses Tackle (Q, gamepad Y, or a tap on the Tackle bar at the bottom centre (UI PR #382)). B's 8 s cooldown and the 20 s same-target
   wait make 20 hits take about 7 minutes; `Debug.tackle(B)` with B facing A runs the same checks with the roll left
   to chance, and `Debug.tackle(B, "loose" or "held")` or `Debug.knockLoose(A)` set up the later rows.
   Expected: while B can tackle, the bar is full and bright orange, with no aim arrow on the ground (UI PR #414);
   the bar hides while B carries a cat or plays the circles or the
   rescue bar. Every press that is not refused lunges B about 12 studs straight ahead in about 0.35 s with a short
   skid, empties the bar, which refills from the left over 8 s and pops once when full, and greys it while B is
   down. A hit stops B, holds B 0.4 s, toasts "Tackled <A's name>!" to B, and pushes A about 10 studs, then holds A
   about 1 s (0.25 plus 0.8); only A's camera bumps. Derek's Set 4 answer 1 asks that a tackled player is knocked
   back a little: watch the push on A's screen, on B's and on a bystander C's, with and without a cat in A's arms, and
   say in the report whether 10 studs reads as "a little". Since F56 (engineer PR #357) every hit past the 3 s grace knocks
   the cat loose (BumpChance 1.0): it drops where A stood as a stray with `Dropped` and `PickUp` true, keeping its
   catId, variant, charm and rescued flag; A sees "You got tackled and dropped your cat! Grab it back!" and B "It
   dropped! Pick it up quick!"; a hit on an empty-handed A toasts "You got tackled!" to A. Anyone picks the cat up
   with the Pick up hold and no circles, B and a third client C as much as A, and with A, B and C holding at once
   exactly one of them carries it ("You picked up a <Cat>!") and the others' holds do nothing; a `GrabBegin` on it
   gets "Hold to pick it up". It adds no charm (reviewer S36) and the new carry is like any other, so it can be
   knocked loose again once 3 s old. If nobody takes it, dusk checks it in for A (F66). `Debug.tackle(B, "held")`
   still gives "It held on tight!" to both, and A cannot be knocked loose for 3 s. A miss (B facing away from A, or
   A more than 18 studs ahead): B lands on the belly and is held 1.2 s, B sees "Missed!", only B gets `ShoveResult`
   "miss" (target 0) and `TackleMissAt` lands on B's character; A sees and hears nothing. A second press before the
   bar refills, hit or miss, does nothing (from the abuse client it is refused "Catch your breath before the next
   tackle"). `Debug.snapshot`: B's `bumpsGiven` counts every press that lunged, `bumpsLanded` every hit, A's
   `bumpsTaken` every hit, and the picker's `pickups` every pick up. The lunge passes through A with no push and no
   toast, and ends as B's miss, for each protection on its own: A within 15 studs of A's door or inside A's plot, A
   in a catch or rescue, B hit A in the last 20 s, anyone hit A in the last 4 s (S45). A press is refused with its
   toast, no lunge and no cooldown spent while B carries ("Check in the cat you are carrying first") and inside 8 s
   of B's last press; `BumpEnabled` false hides the bar and the remote does nothing. Under 3 s into the carry the
   hit still pushes A but never rolls. With A 2 studs to the side of B's line the hit lands (3 studs plus 1.5 of
   slack, root to root); 6 studs to the side it misses.
3. Where a tackle works (F41 as F74; world-and-feel 8.3): a Tier 1 B inside the Park (`Debug.teleport(B, "park")`)
   sees no Tackle bar, and a press from the abuse client is refused "This area opens at a higher tier" with no
   lunge. A and B can tackle each other anywhere in the Alleyway; nobody can tackle on the street, in a cafe or a
   yard (no bar; "You can only tackle inside a catching area"), and a lunge from the Alleyway passes through a
   player just over the line on the street or in the next area. A player in the middle of a catch or rescue is
   passed through and cannot tackle ("Finish what you are doing first", N85). At night every press is refused "The
   areas are closed at night. They open at sunrise.". With two players in reach the nearer one is hit; with three
   players tackling one target, at most one hit lands every 4 s (S45) and the other lunges miss.
4. Rescue lock: A starts a rescue at a trap; B's `RescueBegin` there is refused until A's attempt ends, then
   allowed after 3 s, while A waits 6 s. A walking 15 studs away ends A's attempt as a fail.
5. Announce: A catches an Epic (`Debug.spawnStray("sphynx", "alley")`), finds any gold cat and ranks up to Tier 3.
   Expected: B sees all three announcements. (F14, F30, F24.)
6. Visiting: B walks into A's cafe. Expected: B sees A's working cats in uniform with their names, A's tables and
   A's rich customer when there is one. (F18, F25, F33, F31; P2, record only.)
7. Getaway (v0.8 F55, engineer PR #392; three clients by day in a back alley). A stands beside a stray
   (`Debug.spawnStray("ginger", "alley")`), B 5 studs from A and C 15 studs away; A catches it. Then B starts a
   catch on a second stray right beside A while A catches another; then A rescues a stuck cat (S11) with B
   beside the trap; then A misses a stray on purpose with B beside it; then A picks up a cat B dropped with `G`.
   Expected: on A's catch B is pushed about 8 studs straight away from A, flat (B's own client moves B along the
   `CatchBurst` direction; `PushedAt` on B's character), and C, past CatchBurstRadiusStuds 10, is not. It is not
   a tackle: B keeps a cat B carries, no cooldown is spent, no tackle toast, `bumpsTaken` unchanged. A walks at 24
   (CatchRushWalkSpeed) for 5 s with speed lines (`RushUntil` on A's character), then at carry speed 16, so A
   outruns B and C at 20. Everyone near sees the getaway ring as far as the push (`EffectsChase.spec`) and hears
   `getaway` (A12). B mid-catch beside A is never pushed and B's circles carry on; a rescue pushes like a catch;
   a miss and a pick up push nobody and give no rush.

### 4.4 Abuse pass (server validation; anything accepted is a blocker, owner luau-reviewer)

Run from a client context with the QA LocalScript `tests/abuse/AbuseClient.client.luau`. It only exists in a
build made from `tests.project.json` (`rojo serve tests.project.json`, it lands in
`StarterPlayerScripts.TestsAbuse`) and only runs in Studio. In Play Solo or the second client of a Clients and
Servers session: press ` (backquote) to arm it, the key menu prints, then one key per case, or `]` for every case
in a row. From a client-context command bar: `_G.CatCafeAbuse.run("fakeStray")` or `_G.CatCafeAbuse.runAll()`.
Every line it prints starts with `[Abuse]`; `RESULT <case>: PASS|FAIL|SKIP|CHECK` is the verdict (CHECK: the
client cannot tell, read the server output). After a run, filter the server output for `[Net]`: a
`handler failed` line means the server errored on bad input (a major on its own); a `rate limit hit` line once a
second is the correct reaction to a flood. Remote names are the list in `src/shared/Remotes.luau`. Since F66 the
strays come in one wave at sunrise and the areas close at night (GrabBegin is refused there), so run
`Debug.setPhase("day")` on the server for a fresh wave before the catch cases.

"Coins unchanged" below means unchanged apart from income. Every `ProfileSync` carries settled income and the
server's refusals sync too, so the script asks for a fresh sync first and allows `incomePerSecond` times the
seconds since then, plus one second and one coin for the server's whole-second clock. A spend still shows as a
drop and a second payout as a second sale.

The catch cases (keys 1 to 8, `[`, `;` and `'`) read the `CatchSchedule` the server sent and press on the
synced clock (`Workspace:GetServerTimeNow()`), the same clock the circles are drawn on; in Studio the server prints
a `[Catch]` line for every circle it grades (index, error in ms, grade). Key `[` wants a Rare or rarer stray, whose
catch outlasts the 1 per 2 s `GrabBegin` limit (`Debug.spawnStray("calico")`), and a second stray nearby; key `;`
wants a Common. Before each case the script checks in any cat the last one left in the arms and comes back (a
carrying player's `GrabBegin` gets a toast and no `GrabResult`, laptop run 2026-10-27), and a `GrabBegin` refused
"Too far away" because the stray wandered on gets one more try from beside it. Rows marked (manual) are run by
hand from the command bar with `Remotes.event(name):FireServer(...)`.

Catching and rescue (F26, F27, F23)

| Case | Call | Expected server behaviour |
|---|---|---|
| Fake stray (key 1) | `GrabBegin("not_a_stray")`, `CatchPress("not_a_stray", 1)` | Ignored, no answer, no error in console |
| Grab limit (key `[`) | `GrabBegin` on a second stray within 1 s of the first | Dropped by the 1 per 2 s limit: no answer, no schedule |
| Same stray again (key `[`) | `GrabBegin(id)` 2 s into its own catch | `GrabResult` `Keep going, the circles are coming`, no second schedule, one `CatchResult` |
| One catch at a time (key `[`) | 2 s into a catch, stand by another stray and `GrabBegin` it | `Finish this catch first`, no schedule |
| Retry wait (key `;`) | Miss a Common on purpose and `GrabBegin(id)` while it runs; miss it again and `GrabBegin(id)` in the second after it stops; then once more, pressing on target | Each `CatchResult` success false with no `pawsLeft`; refused `It's running!` while `FleeingUntil` is ahead; refused `Catch your breath, then try again` for the player who missed during StraySelfRetrySeconds 1 after the stop (S44); then a new schedule, and the on-target presses catch it. The case spaces its `GrabBegin`s 2 s apart for the rate limit, so it needs the second miss. Another player gets a schedule at once after the stop (section 4.3 item 1b) |
| Unlimited tries (key `.`) | Miss one stray three times (a Legendary if one is near: `Debug.spawnStray("void")`), each `GrabBegin` once it has stopped and the extra second is over, then `GrabBegin(id)` a fourth time | Every `GrabBegin` starts a catch, none is refused for trust; the stray stays, with no `Patience` or `Paws_<UserId>` attribute on its model |
| Far grab (key 2) | Teleport 200 studs away, `GrabBegin(id)` | Refused with `Too far away`, no schedule |
| Far press (key 8) | `GrabBegin` in range, teleport 200 studs away, press every circle on target | Not caught although every press is on target. Walking out of range is a Miss with the toast `Too far away`: the cat sprints off with `FleeingUntil` set, as after any Miss |
| Locked area | Tier 1, teleport past the Park gate, `GrabBegin(park stray)`, `RescueBegin(park trap)` | Refused, nothing locked, no schedule or challenge |
| Pick up race (F56; Clients and Servers, 3 players) | After B tackles A loose, A, B and C each fire `PickUpCat(strayId)` in the same frame from beside the cat (the strayId is the model's `StrayId` attribute); then C fires `GrabBegin` on another Dropped cat, and `PickUpCat` on an ordinary stray | Exactly one of the three carries the cat; the other two get no carry, and the cat is gone from the world, so a late call does nothing. `GrabBegin` on a Dropped cat answers "Hold to pick it up" with no circles; `PickUpCat` on an ordinary stray answers "Catch this one with the circles". No `[Net] PickUpCat handler failed` line |

Expected: the server's exploit check flags it from its own stamped errors (Perfect rate above 95% over the last 50 circles; no `TelemetryReport` involved), the warn `[CatService] autopress_flag set for <userId>`. (v0.4) While flagged, `charmGain` is 0: key 7 fails a Purrfect catch that earns charm after 50 Perfect circles in a row. Then press 8 of 10 circles late (Good) for 50 circles and the flag clears at 85% or below (warn `autopress_flag cleared for <userId>`, an analytics event once analytics exists). Flag it, rejoin (API access on) and play 10 Good circles: still flagged, since a buffer under 50 never clears a saved flag (N46). Log, do not ticket, unless the flag never fires or never clears |

### 4.5 Performance (phone emulator, P2, record only)

`execute_luau` on the client: average `RunService.Heartbeat` delta over 10 s in the cafe with customers on and
off (hook or a client attribute), and during a five-circle catch. Budget from F15: customers under 2 ms per frame;
the whole scene under 16.7 ms on the emulator's default quality. Record `Stats:GetTotalMemoryUsageMb()` at join and
after 10 minutes, and the count of gold sparkles (ParticleEmitters named `shiny` on cat bodies: at most `goldEmitters`, 3 on a phone
within 80 studs and 8 on a PC within 110). (v0.4, F37, world-and-feel 8.8) Standing in the street
with eight running cafes on a phone-sized viewport: 30 or more frames per second; cafes beyond 80 studs culled and
roaming paused with nobody within 60 studs.
(Effects v1, reviewer S35 and the 2026-10-27 build review) At night on the phone emulator with all five areas
spawned (50 strays; hook `Debug.setPhase("night")` at Tier 5 so every gate is open), stand where the most strays
are in view and open the MicroProfiler (Ctrl+F6, or Settings, MicroProfiler): record the client frame time over
10 s and the share under the Effects labels. Target: the frame stays under 33 ms (30 frames per second) and no more
than 8 cats move on the client at once (the low budget). Record, do not ticket, unless the frame rate drops below
30.

### 4.6 Effects (v0.4 and v0.5, effects list "Effects v1" and "Effects v1.1", world-and-feel 7)

Precondition: `Main.client.luau` calls `Effects.start()` (on main since engineer PR #85). On an older build mark
every row skipped with that reason. Run on PC, then repeat E1, E2 and E6 on the phone emulator. Watch
the client output for anything from `Effects` through the whole section (E8). Every effect lives on this client
only: client parts go in `workspace.ClientEffects` and burst emitters on `Burst_*` attachments under
`workspace.Terrain`; neither exists on the server (server `execute_luau`: both absent).

The player who missed gets one small camera shake (about 0.18 s); the watcher sees the puff, "!", gallop and pant (from `HissAt` and `FleeingUntil`) but no shake. Since VFX PR #543 (Derek 12:13) it shows only in view: a wall, a building or the far side of a rock hides it as it hides the trap, walking round into view pops it up, turning the camera away and back pops it again, a trap seen through a window or leaves still shows, and a lamp post or a player passing in front never makes it pop (it waits half a second). Reduce Motion: it appears with no pop or bob. It goes once you are within 10 studs, where the Rescue card takes over, and standing on that edge never flickers it; it hides for everyone while someone rescues that cat, and goes when the cat is rescued or the trap empties at nightfall |

### 4.7 Sound (v0.4 and v0.5, sound list v0.4, `src/client/Audio`)

Run with the sound setting on, PC first, in the same session as 4.6 so each effect and its sound are judged
together. Every Sound the client plays is named after its cue (`catHiss`, `purr`, `shuttersUp`...), so client
`execute_luau` can list the playing ones: descendants of `workspace` and `SoundService` with `IsA("Sound")` and
`IsPlaying`. Since PR #160 (44 files) and audio PR #338 (32 more, 2026-10-27 21:55) every cue the rows below name has
an uploaded id, so a cue that stays silent where its row expects it is a fail. Since Sound-Agent PRs #362 and #370 (Derek
22:34) there is no ambience at all: the cafe, Alleyway and Park beds and the cafe door loop are silent on purpose,
and three quiet music tracks have files in their place (A13). A cue whose `ids` list in `Audio/Cues.luau` is
still empty (the ambience beds and world loops, `musicNightB`, the Rare to Mythical catch jingles and the food cues
among them) is recorded as skipped, not failed; a cue with a `sheet` plays a stretch of its sheet's file, so its empty
`ids` is no skip (the rescue cues since Sound-Agent PR #765, A23). New ids reach the live game only with a publish, so judge them
in a Studio build of `main` at 8c2562d or later.

| Row | Do | Expected |
|---|---|---|

## 5. Manual checklist (Derek first, then friends)

Run on a phone preset and once on PC. Tick each line, note the build date. Placeholders (`PH_` parts, Part cats,
colored Part uniforms) are expected in the rough draft and are cosmetic tickets only.

Core loop (features.md section 2 done-when lines)
- [ ] F01 Join, own a plot, walk out of the back door to a stray in under 2 s (v0.4; before F35 cafe to alley and back in under 60 s)
- [ ] F19 (HUD v3) My Cafe (or H), Alley and Areas on the bottom row work from anywhere; the Areas panel lists only the areas your tier has opened, with no spawn rates; all grey while carrying, with My Cafe reading "Walk it home" until you check in or drop the cat
- [ ] F22 A v2 save loads as v3: tier 1, cafe level and park pass refunded, two cats working, no coins lost (API access on, an old dev save)
- [ ] F23 and F66 Day 5 minutes and a 90 s night, the first day a full 5 minutes; a wave in each open area at every sunrise (6, 5, 5, 4 and 4 strays); at nightfall every stray and stuck cat goes, the areas close and you are walked home; locked area gates hold
- [ ] F24 Rank up card ticks each requirement; Tier 2 with 1,500 coins and 5 different cats, refused with 4; income rises 25%; the Park gate opens; each tier opens its slots and shop caps
- [ ] F25 In the cat room pick a Tabby and tap a free Barista "+": it stands upright at the counter in an apron and barista cap and earns 1.5x; resting cats stay on all fours with nothing on; Equip Best fills every open slot and Unequip All rests them all; at night the upright cats doze standing and wake at dawn
- [ ] F26 Circles: one slow circle for a Common, five uneven ones for a Mythical with a Basic Net
- [ ] F34 and F40: any mix of Good and Perfect always catches; a Miss ends the catch at once and the cat sprints off, its prompt hidden until it stops; you can chase it and try again as often as you like, with no paw row; an all-Perfect catch shows the Purrfect burst and "Charm +N"
- [ ] F35 and F77 The Alleyway is right behind the cafes, both back alleys feel roomy and are closed at both ends; rarer cats wait in the nooks at the alley ends

Test by joining a v3 save with a v2 build, or with an engineer hook that writes `version = 99` to a dev profile

## 6. Ten minute loop script (Phase 2 exit test) with expected numbers

Fresh account, phone preset, timer running. The steps are features.md section 8; the numbers come from
data-model v0.3 and become spec checks as each feature lands.

1. Join. The intro plays once (25.5 s, Skip after 5 s) and leaves you inside your back door (F72). Plot owned, Tabby working as Barista in an apron and barista cap, HUD `$50` and `1.5/s` within 5 s (Common 1/s, charm 50,
   favorite role 1.5x, tier 1.0, day 1.0).
2. Walk out of the back door into the Alleyway. A Common stray waits right behind your cafe even by day
   (tutorial guarantee below step 2). Hold E for half a second, then one slow circle (1.40 s, Basic Net) pressed
   with Space. Good or Perfect catches it; a Miss
   sends it sprinting about 12 studs, so follow it and try again once it stops.
3. Walk home at speed 16 (13 before F35), check in at either door. Cat found popup, `+50` reward, book `2 / 50`.
4. Assign the new cat as Waiter. It stands up in a bow tie and a vest. Rate rises by 0.8 to 1.2 (charm), or 1.2 to 1.8 for a
   Tuxedo or Snow, so the HUD reads between `2.3/s` and `3.3/s`.
5. Buy Table 1 at `$150` (rate x1.10), then the Bait bowl at `$600` (v0.13.6), then Kibble at `$30` (v0.13.1) and put it in from the F menu. The
   countdown shows `10:00`. The tutorial strip reads "Buy the Bait bowl in Upgrades (U)" after the table, then
   "Put Kibble in your bowl on the back step (F)" with the guide marker on the bowl, and goes away with the first
   food (F13, PR #147).
6. By day the Alleyway holds the sunrise wave: six strays and a stuck cat in each of the three traps with chance
   0.6, at least one always (RescueMinStuck). Rescue it with the bar. The first night comes 5 minutes after the
   server opened (F66): the areas close, the rate stays the same (F60) and the cafe closes until sunrise 90 s later.
   From Table 1 on a tip pile comes to a table every 30 s by day (F61); walk past them to collect.
7. Free play until minute 10. Expected: three or four different cats, two roles filled, two tables, a bait cat on
   the way, the rank up card showing what Tier 2 still needs (8,000 coins and 5 different cats; since v0.13.6 the cats come
   first, at about minute 9, and the coins later).
8. Leave for 10 minutes, rejoin. Offline popup shows `floor(dayRate x 0.25 x 600)`, for example `412` at a day rate
   of `2.75/s`, and the bait cat is waiting at the bowl. Tame it.
9. Pass: steps 1 to 7 under 10 minutes, no console errors, no open blocker tickets. Analytics (F16, engineer PRs
   #155 and #156; analytics-plan section 9): on the server,
   `print(table.concat(game.ServerStorage.CatCafeDebug:Invoke("telemetry", 40), "\n"))` lists the onboarding steps
   `joined` (with `[pc]` in Studio, never `[unknown]`), `saw_alley`, `first_catch`, `first_checkin`, `first_role`,
   `first_table`, `first_bait` and, after the bait cat, `first_bait_resolved`, once each and in that order, and a
   `source tips` line right before any `sink` made while tips were pending; `CatCafeDebug:Invoke("onboarding")`
   shows the step reached with nothing held. On a second fresh profile, rank up to Tier 2 through the Rank up card
   before any bait (`setCoins` and `giveCat` to qualify; `setTier` skips analytics): `onboarding` shows step 9 held
   and the log has no `tier_2` line until `first_bait_resolved` is logged, then both in order. A `[Telemetry]` warn
   means an AnalyticsService call failed and was swallowed: a ticket, not a crash.

Extra checks for v0.3 to v0.5 (features.md section 8): the tackle test over 20 hits (section 4.3 item 2); any mix
of Good and Perfect always catches and a Miss always ends the attempt (C7 and S4; before F34, all-Perfect and
all-Good give 100% and 50%); a missed cat sprints and can be retried without limit (section 4.3 item 1b); travel greyed while carrying (S20); For Rent
plots on a near-empty server (S21); the dealer stock is identical after a
rejoin or a server change in the same window (section 4.4; v0.5: for every player); Tier 2 is reached in 10 to 20 minutes of normal play (time it on two
fresh accounts and record the minute). `Pace.spec` re-timed it with F60 and F61 on 2026-10-28: the median of 200
seeded runs is 15.4 minutes at 4 catches a day and 14.8 at 5 (10th to 90th about 8 to 21), with the 5 cats ready
at about 9 to 10 minutes, so the coins come last as v0.13.6 wants; a player who buys only the tables and the bait
bowl before ranking up gets there in about 12 (11.6 to 12.8). The data-model 11 model's 12 minutes for F60 and F61
matches that player. Both sit inside the window, so the tier costs stay. On 2026-10-28 (data-model v0.13.15, F83)
`Pace.spec` started netting each stray with presses spread about 50 ms, so misses and chases cost their real time.
On the build before F83 about 3% of tries miss and Tier 2 stays at about 15 minutes (12 for the saver). With F83's
`catchWindow` patched in by hand (0.9 to 0.5) about 8% of tries miss, the chases cost about 8 s a run and Tier 2
still lands at about 15 (12 for the saver); at 70 ms presses about a quarter of tries miss and it is still about
15, and at 100 ms about 15 with the cats ready at 11. Tier 2 waits on coins, not on catching, because Alleyway
strays are mostly Commons and Rares. A net at 0.15 of its windows takes it to 25 to 30 minutes and fails the check.
F86 (v0.13.16) cannot move Tier 2: until Tier 2 the Alleyway is the newest area, so no cat is older. The design's
model puts Tier 5 at 2 h 14 with it (2 h 21 before); QA checks F86 through the rates it pays (S60 and the F86
spec rows) rather than a two hour run, and F85's longer carries cost time on Tier 3 and up, never coins.

F95, the money ladder (2026-11-02, on main after #656): `Pace.spec` now runs the same player on to Tier 5 with five
second steps, a settle and a look at the shop every 30 s, and Equip best only when a new cat can win a slot (the
skipped plans change nothing: the 2-run results were identical with and without the skip). The gate uses 10 seeded
runs a player; a 20-run measurement gave these medians:

| player | Tier 2 | Tier 3 | Tier 4 | Tier 5 | target (economy-ladder.md) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| one go, 4 catches a day | 16 min | 48 min | 3 h 05 | 9 h 03 (10th to 90th 7 h 36 to 10 h 34) | 8 to 12 h (design model 10 h) |
| an hour a day, free offline | 16 min (day 1) | 48 min (day 1) | 2 h 00 (day 3) | 4 h 15 (day 5; 10th to 90th 4 h to 6 h) | day 4 to 6 (design day 5) |
| 4x Money, one go | 8 min | 30 min | 1 h 24 | 4 h 21 (10th 2 h 49) | over 2 h (design about 4 h 45) |
| every paid bonus (24x, no extra slot), one go | 11 min | 30 min | 58 min | 1 h 46 (10th 1 h 23) | over 1 h (design 2 h 08 with the slot) |

All four hold, and the Tier 2 checks still give about 16 minutes (13 for the saver). The run is a little faster than
the design's model because it claims every Index milestone (tips forever and bait speed) and feeds the bowl better
food as income grows. Lowering the Tier 5 cost to 200M by hand takes one-go play to 5 h 30 and fails the check, so it
bites. The extra role slot is not wired into EquipBest yet, so the 24x player is a slot short of the real one; when it
is, the row is re-run. The long runs add about 70 s to `npm run check`.

F97 and the F95 v2 costs (2026-11-02, on main after #714 and #723): the model now pays each tip pile the moment it
appears, prices food with `Economy.foodPrice` at the player's tier and picks each trip's stray from a sunrise wave
that holds an Epic or rarer (`Catching.waveRare`). With the old costs F97 took one-go play to Tier 5 in 7 h 00 and
the saver to Tier 2 in 10.0 minutes, both fails; the Epic in every wave did it (9 h 34 without it), not the dearer
food. Design-Agent raised the costs to Tier 2 10,000, Tier 3 500,000, Tier 4 40M and Tier 5 1.2B (#719) and all
seven checks pass. A 20-run measurement on main gave these medians:

| player | Tier 2 | Tier 3 | Tier 4 | Tier 5 | target (economy-ladder.md) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| one go, 4 catches a day | 14 min | 40 min | 2 h 42 | 9 h 09 (10th to 90th 7 h 49 to 10 h 51) | 8 to 12 h |
| an hour a day, free offline | 14 min (day 1) | 40 min (day 1) | 1 h 27 (day 2) | 4 h 00 (day 5; 10th to 90th 3 h 26 to 5 h 00) | day 4 to 6 |
| 4x Money, one go | 9 min | 25 min | 1 h 18 | 4 h 13 (10th to 90th 3 h 13 to 4 h 34) | over 2 h |
| every paid bonus (24x, no extra slot), one go | 7 min | 25 min | 49 min | 1 h 41 (10th to 90th 1 h 30 to 2 h 14) | over 1 h |

The 40-run Tier 2 checks give 12.7 minutes at 4 catches a day (cats ready at 8.8), 12.5 for the saver and 15.3 for
the shakier player, all inside 10 to 20. The 24x row is still a slot short until EquipBest reads the extra slot.

F88, Luck (2026-11-02, engineer PR #743): the model buys the Lucky Cat from the shop like any upgrade, puts its Luck
in each food's roll when the food goes in (`BaitService.onUseLure`), and joins servers at a random point of their
four day run, so a Lucky Day's sunrise wave rolls at LuckyDayWaveLuck x2 and its food gets x0.5 more. Potions stay
out. 20-run medians on the engineer's branch, with Luck modelled (and without it, the statue bought for nothing):

| player | Tier 5 | target |
|---|---|---|
| one go, 4 catches a day | 8 h 53 (10th to 90th 7 h 41 to 9 h 33; without 9 h 25) | 8 to 12 h |
| an hour a day, free offline | day 5, 4 h 00 of play (without 4 h 30) | day 4 to 6 |
| 4x Money, one go | 4 h 03 (without 3 h 55) | over 2 h |
| every paid bonus (24x, no extra slot), one go | 1 h 41 (without 1 h 41) | over 1 h |

Tier 2 at 4 catches a day takes 12.1 minutes, the shakier player 13.8 and the saver 10.9, the closest of all to its
10 minute floor: if a later change takes the saver under 10, the Tier 2 cost goes up.

F99, the Rainbow coat (2026-11-02, engineer PR #795): the model rolls each cat's coat with `Catching.rollCoat`,
rainbow first and then gold on the same roll (one draw, as the gold roll was), records the rainbow collection and
claims the Rainbow page with the rest. A rainbow cat earns RainbowMultiplier 3 at 0.5% a catch, and the 20-run
medians move within run to run noise: one go 9 h 05 (10th to 90th 6 h 55 to 9 h 56; 8 h 53 without), an hour a day
day 5 after 4 h 00, 4x Money 4 h 11 (4 h 03 without), 24x 1 h 41 (the same), and Tier 2 unchanged (saver 10.9
minutes). Every target holds.

F84, the Cat Cannon trip (2026-11-02, after engineer PR #871): until now each trip was the old 45 s out the back
door and back, and the tutorial cat was checked in at 75 s. `Pace.spec` now times hometown-cannon.md 6a's trip:
5 s to the Town Square cannon, a 0.5 s hop and the 3 s flight, the walk from the camp's pad to a spawn point the
stray's rarity may take in the area's set (`Catching.spawnPool` on Layout's points, so F85's long sets count as each
lands) along a path 1.25 times the straight line, the carry back with the 10 s catch rush and the 1.6 s cloud hop
home; the tutorial cat is in at 51 s. An Alleyway trip is about 25 s and a deep Sewer one about a minute. Over 200
seeded runs a player that took about a minute off Tier 2 and put the saver at 5 catches a day under the floor: 9.8
minutes (10.3 with the old trip). By rule v0.8 Design-Agent raised the Tier 2 cost from 10,000 to 12,000
(economy-ladder.md v2.1, engineer PR #871). At 12,000 the 200-run Tier 2 medians are 13.5 minutes at 4 catches a
day, 11.9 at 5, 11.1 and 10.2 for the saver and 14.5 for the shakier player; the gate's 40 runs give 13.6, 11.3,
10.6, 10.4 and 15.7. Tier 5 holds on the gate's 10 runs: one go 10 h 01 (10th to 90th 8 h 32 to 11 h 12), an hour
a day day 5 after 4 h 00, 4x Money 3 h 38 and every paid bonus 1 h 31. The saver's Tier 2 hardly moves with the
price (9.8, 10.0 and 10.2 minutes at 10,000, 11,000 and 12,000), so if it crosses 10 again the lever is the cats or
the bowl more than the cost.

## 7. Reporting

One file per run, `docs/playtests/YYYY-MM-DD.md`, in the `playtest-report` format: summary, bugs, balance,
confusion, requests, quotes. Tickets use the skill's severity scale (blocker, major, minor, cosmetic), carry
repro steps, expected, actual, evidence path and an owner (engineer, ui-designer, asset-artist, designer). Exploit
shaped findings go to luau-reviewer as blockers. QA never writes the fix.

## 9. Known gaps

F54 is on main (engineer PR #325, UI PRs #301 and #332). Both tickets from the QA pass on main 4c2dc2f,
2026-10-27, are fixed: the full All Cats track's bait wait of 371 s, not the design's 6:12 (372 s), from the ten
bait speeds summing to 0.38000000000000012 before `Economy.baitWaitSeconds` floored (engineer PR #335; QA's walk
over every claim with and without the Big Bowl is in `IndexRewardsShared.spec`), and gold-track and All Cats
cards showing a gift chip reading "x0" instead of their forever bonus (UI PR #332). Settled: buying the Big Bowl never moves food already
in the bowl; only a bowl saved without a `readyAt` gets one (Gameplay-Engineer, 2026-10-27, S40).

The cloud harness cannot load modules that touch `Instance.new`, events, DataStores, TextService or
`RunService:IsServer()`, and its type check treats most `Enum` types loosely; `./check.sh` with luau-lsp is the
truth for types. Text filtering only behaves for real on live servers. Nothing here replaces a human playing on a
real phone for ten minutes.
